$ gcloud artifacts packages list \
  --project=class-6-5-tiqs \
  --location=us-central1 \
  --repository=vertex-agent-lab \
  --format='value(name)' \
| tr -d '\r' \
| while IFS= read -r package; do
    [ -z "${package}" ] && continue
    gcloud artifacts packages delete "${package##*/}" \
      --project=class-6-5-tiqs \
      --location=us-central1 \
      --repository=vertex-agent-lab \
      --quiet
  done
